Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the metals object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 126. The moduli space has dimension at most 328 with 2 connected components.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by metals generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 126, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 328. QED.
